MSCI 降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待
2026 年 10 月 27 日
一、 市場動態
1. MSCI 擬將印尼降至獨立市場，降評風險明顯升高：MSCI 於 10 月 15 日宣布，啟動
將印尼股市由「新興市場（Emerging Market）」 直接降至「獨立市場（Standalone
Market）」的諮詢程序，意見徵詢將於 2027 年 4 月 30 日截止，最晚於 2027 年 6 月
30 日公布最終決定。MSCI 原先於今年 1 月提出可能降至「邊境市場（Frontier Market
）」，此次則跳過「邊境市場」提出直接將該國股市降至「獨立市場」的方案，顯示對

印尼市場治理的疑慮進一步加深。雖然「邊境市場」仍可能是替代分類方案，但 MSCI
強調，除非有明確證據顯示市場治理及運作出現實質改善，否則預期將依目前提案重
新分類，顯示此次降評風險較過去顯著升高。
2. 股權透明度與交易秩序問題為 MSCI 主要疑慮：MSCI 指出，印尼股市長期存在股權
結構不透明、實際自由流通股比例難以確認、疑似協調交易，以及市場監理執法成效
不足等問題，影響國際投資人對市場公平性與可投資性的信心。雖然印尼主管機關已
提出強化持股超過 1%股東資訊揭露、建立高持股集中度（HSC）監理機制，以及規
劃逐步將最低自由流通股比例提高至 15%等改革措施，但 MSCI 認為相關改革尚未展
現持續且具實質性的改善成果。由於問題涉及市場制度與治理結構，改善所需時間可
能較一般短期市場波動更長。
3. 股市反彈後再度回落，外資撤離壓力仍高：受降評消息衝擊，印尼雅加達綜合指數 10
月 16 日重挫 11.57%，創 1998 年以來最大單日跌幅，19 日再跌 3.64%，盤中最低

觸及 4,907.37 點。雖然 20 日至 23 日連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度下跌 1.22%
，收於 5,373.09 點，各產業類股全面收黑，顯示市場信心仍不穩定。根據 Reuters
報導，截至 10 月 19 日，2026 年外資已累計淨賣超印尼股票約 48.8 億美元。此外，
印尼證交所 26 日宣布，將引進獨立專家及外國投資人代表參與高持股集中度審查，
並自 11 月起加強調查疑似協調交易，必要時凍結可疑帳戶，以回應 MSCI 對市場制
度的疑慮。
二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 股市：潛在指數剔除與資金外流，持續壓抑市場評價
若印尼股市最終降至「獨立市場」，原則上將不再納入 MSCI 新興市場及邊境市場相關
指數，追蹤相關指數的被動式基金將須依規則調整持股，實際處理方式仍以 MSCI 後
續公告為準。若改降至「邊境市場」，則可能獲得部分邊境市場資金承接，衝擊相對較
小。Reuters 引述多家金融機構估計，印尼股市降至獨立市場可能引發約 30 億至 60

不另行通知。
億美元被動資金流出，其中 UBS 估計約 40 億美元，並指出主動型基金另有約 150 億
美元潛在撤資風險，惟實際規模仍視最終分類結果及基金策略而定。
2. 匯率：外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力
印尼股市的降評風險不僅衝擊股市，也可能透過外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力。印尼
盾 10 月 16 日盤中一度貶至 18,070 兌 1 美元，為 7 月下旬以來最弱水準之一，當日
收盤約 18,000。若後續國際資金持續流出，可能進一步增加股匯市波動，對以外幣計
價、且未完全避險的印尼基金及 ETF 形成額外壓力。雖然印尼央行可透過匯市干預及

流動性管理措施穩定金融市場，但在資金外流壓力尚未緩解前，印尼盾仍可能維持偏
弱格局。
3. 基金與 ETF：指數權重下滑，單一市場曝險風險升高
MSCI 相關指數調整已對印尼股票產生實質影響。Reuters 引用 LSEG 資料顯示，印尼
在 MSCI 新興市場指數的權重已由 2026 年初的 1.16%降至 9 月底的 0.43%，相關成
分股亦由年初 18 檔減至 9 檔，反映部分指數資金調整壓力已提前出現。對印尼單一
國家基金及 ETF 而言，主要風險來自股市下跌、資金流出及印尼盾波動；主動式基金
雖可透過選股降低部分影響，但仍難以完全迴避整體市場風險。不同產品實際受到的
影響，仍應依追蹤指數、主要持股及匯率避險安排個別評估。
三、 市場看法：
1. 制度性問題難以速解，降評風險將持續牽動市場：我們認為，此次 MSCI 降評風險並
非單純反映經濟成長放緩或企業獲利不佳，而是涉及市場透明度、交易公平性與監理
執法等結構性問題。尤其 MSCI 不僅啟動降評諮詢，更持續限制印尼股票的部分指數

調整，包括不提高外資納入因子、不新增相關成分股，以及不進行向上規模分類調整
，顯示市場可投資性疑慮尚未獲得有效解決。儘管政府試圖推動改革，但制度修正仍
須經過執行與成效驗證，才能重新取得國際投資人的信任，因此短期內降評風險仍難
完全消除。
2. 外資撤離與指數權重下降，市場信心修復恐需較長時間：印尼今年以來外資已大幅賣
超，MSCI 新興市場指數中的權重亦明顯下降，顯示國際投資人對當地市場的疑慮已
逐漸反映於實際資金流向。我們認為，即使印尼最終成功避免降評，部分國際機構投
資人仍可能因市場透明度、流動性及治理品質疑慮而維持較低曝險。此外，MSCI 對
部分指數調整的凍結措施尚未解除，短期內新增指數資金流入機會仍受到限制，預期
國際資金回流與市場評價修復均可能需要較長時間。
3. 監理改革力度加大，但尚不足以扭轉保守展望：印尼政府近期持續加強改革，包括提
高資訊透明度、強化交易監控、對違規交易開罰，以及引進外部獨立人士參與監理審
查。10 月 26 日該國政府進一步提出主動調查疑似協調交易及凍結可疑帳戶等措施，

11 月 2 日起亦規劃將高持股集中度股票排除於雅加達綜合指數之外。相關改革有助改

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
善交易秩序及短期市場信心，但部分措施仍待正式實施，且實際成效尚未獲得 MSCI
認可。考量制度性問題改善需要時間，政策利多雖可能帶動短線反彈，但尚不足以改
變對印尼股市中期前景的保守判斷。
4. 股市跌深不代表利空出盡，中期風險報酬吸引力偏低：印尼股市在 10 月 16 日及 19
日大幅修正後，雖曾連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度回落，顯示市場尚未形成穩
定回升趨勢。我們認為，當前股市面臨的不僅是短期賣壓，更包括國際投資人信任下
降及風險溢價提高，即使企業基本面未同步惡化，仍可能因評價下修而承受股價壓力

。因此，短線不排除政策利多或跌深後出現技術性反彈，但在 MSCI 態度、外資流向
與印尼盾走勢明顯改善前，反彈延續性仍有疑慮，印尼股市中期展望維持保守。
四、市場展望：
1. 印尼股市維持保守展望，單一市場風險報酬吸引力不足：考量 MSCI 降評風險尚未解除、
外資撤離壓力持續，以及市場治理改革仍有待驗證，我們認為印尼股市未來數季的風險報
酬吸引力相對有限。即使近期股價明顯回落，仍難認定相關風險已充分反映。相較市場制
度成熟、國際資金流動性較佳的新興市場，印尼股市可能持續面臨較高風險溢價，因此對
單一印尼市場的集中曝險應維持審慎態度。
2. 此事件對新興市場指數與東協指數影響相對有限：根據 MSCI 資料，截至 2026 年 9 月底
，印尼股市在 MSCI Emerging Market 指數與 MSCI AC ASEAN 指數中的權重分別約為
0.43%與 7.55%，因此我們認為印尼潛在降評對相關指數的直接衝擊相對有限。此外，新
興市場各國經濟結構與產業組成有所差異，區域型基金可透過跨國分散降低單一市場風險

。惟後續仍須留意印尼事件對整體新興市場投資信心造成的外溢影響，以及全球貿易與匯
率波動風險。

Aspeed (5274.TWO): Mgmt. visit: BMC and SMC specification migration 
and customers’ expansion to drive growth ahead; Buy
16 November 2026 | 11:05PM HKT
We visited Aspeed in Taipei recently. Management remain positive on future growth, 
riding on further customer expansion, product mix upgrade, and their continuous 
new product launches. The company highlights its comprehensive coverage across 
various chipset platforms (e.g. GPU, ASIC, RISC-V, etc.) and coverage across different 
layers in AI data center (e.g. component-level, server-level, rack-level, data 
center-level), securing their leading market position. We remain positive on Aspeed 
and see the growing servers demand along with AI servers design getting more 
complicated to support the company’s growth ahead. Maintain Buy. Read more: 
Aspeed Oct revenues and 3Q26 results in line. 
Key takeaways 
1. Positive tone on future growth: Management remain positive on future business 
growth, driven by further customer expansion, product mix upgrade, and their 
continuous new product launches. The company’s shipment is mainly from BMC2600 
in 2026, and 1700 and 2700 would further expand shipment in 2027, carrying higher 
ASP as stronger specification (e.g. integrated more functions, I/O supporting DDR5). 
The company would also have 2705 prior to 2800, which offers stronger security. The 
2800 is targeted to start sampling in 2028, and would be a driver beyond 2028, 
integrating more functions, stronger security (e.g. silicon-level security), stronger I/O, 
and using more advanced processing nodes. On SMC and Platform, the company will 
also have new products entering mass production in 2H27: (1) SMC 1040 and 1840 
(embedded FPGA, serving both single-node and multi-node), and (2) platform 1080, 
aiming to further expand customers. The company’s pure exposure to the server 
market also brings it strong end market demand, with the ability to pass through the 
higher raw material costs to end customers (e.g. higher costs from substrate, OSAT, 
foundry), supporting its GM. 
2. Competition advantages: Management highlight they are early in the market and 
serve various customers (e.g. GPU, ASIC, RISC-V, etc.), with comprehensive product 
coverage across different SoC platforms, and the company is also expanding its 
product portfolio vertically: across SMC (component-level mgmt.), BMC 
(server-level), and RMC (rack-level). The company’s products can monitor various 
scenarios, such as GPU thermal issue, liquid cooling leakage, etc. Management also 
highlight their large R&D team (over 100 engineers), local and timely support (having 
R&D team in Taiwan, close to customers), and ecosystem (work closely with ODMs). 
We remain positive on Aspeed’s leading market position and see AI server 
specifications getting more and more complicated (e.g. more shelves, more chips per

### Page 2

rack, from air cooling to liquid cooling, etc.), which would further enhance Aspeed’s 
competition edges. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Aspeed (5274.TWO) 
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$22,000 is based on a target P/E of 40x (in line with 
its 10-year historical trading average) applied to our 2028E EPS, and discounted back to 
2027E at 13.6% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.5x beta (sourcing 
Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 6.25% (in line 
with GS house view). 
Key downside risks to our view and TP: 1) A softer-than-expected server market 
demand recovery, 2) slower-than-expected BMC penetration within AI servers, 3) 
intensifying market competition.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 16 Nov 2026 close.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Summary: Merida hosted its 3Q26 investor call on 17 November after market hours, 
following release of its 3Q26 results on 11 November (see our memo). Merida’s gross 
margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our estimate 
(16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%). Factoring in the equity investment loss of 
TWD150m+, its 3Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the 
consensus estimate (TWD1.65).

We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. For more information on this company, please 
refer to our latest flash note, 2Q26 review: mainly dragged by Specialized, on 14 August.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results recap. Merida’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD6,098m (-12.1% QoQ, -15.6% 
YoY) missed our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates by 13.6% and 11.1%, 
respectively, which we attributed to the discount when destocking the older models. Its 
gross margin came in at 15.2% (vs. 19.8% in 2Q26; 13.7% in 3Q25), lower than our 
estimate (16.2%) and the consensus (17.3%), due to less OBM sales, the price discount 
for further inventory digestion and the depreciation of the Euro. Factoring in the equity 
investment loss of TWD150m+, its net profit of TWD176m (-44.8% QoQ, -71.9% YoY) 
missed our and the consensus estimates by 65.0% and 65.6%. Its 3Q26 EPS was 
TWD1.06, lower than our forecast (TWD1.68) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.65).  
• 2026 outlook update. Merida revised down its 2026 full-year shipments to 960k units 
(Taiwan plant 280k, down from previous 340k; China plant 680k, down from previous 
700k), as it continues to manage its existing model shipments and expects new models 
in 2027 to bring stronger demand. The company now sees recovery in Europe and 
China, with inventory digestion coming to an end: 9M26 sales in Europe grew 14% YoY, 
driven by strong demand for road bikes and gravel bikes; 9M 26 sales in China grew by 
1% while shipments grew by 7% (vs. revenue guidance of declining 15-20% at the 
beginning of 2026), driven by low- and mid-end models. 
• SBC expected to be profitable in 4Q26. Due to SBC’s conservative tax assumption 
(over 100% effective tax rate) in 1Q-3Q26, if and when the tax rate realised at year-end 
is determined to be less than currently allocated for, we expect there will be a huge profit 
recognised in 4Q26 to offset the estimated after-tax loss recognised in 1Q-3Q26. Merida 
and SBC will review the tax assumption policy after full-year results are out, as the 
policy has caused a great deal of investor confusion. Including USD15m of tariff refund 
in 3Q26, SBC’s pre-tax profit grew by 42% YoY for 9M26. Merida expects there is 
another USD5m of tariff refund to collect in 4Q26.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD96, based on a target PER of 17x 
applied on our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 
13.2x/10.6x, based on our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-5-year one-year-forward PER 
trading range of 7-33x. Key downside risks: slower-than-expected e-bike penetration in the 
EU and the US, worse-than-expected demand in an inflationary environment and inventory 
digestion.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

ab 23 November 2026
Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
ITEQ Corporation
Potential levers with upside; Initiate at Buy
Initiate on TW CCL  - ITEQ: Riding CCL tide with more upside if M9+ materializes
We initiate coverage of ITEQ with a Buy rating and PT of NT$650. We believe ITEQ is well
positioned to benefit from the  CCL upcycle and AI infrastructure buildout due to its: 1)
leading position in general-server CCL and exposure to increasing M7 adoption; 2) rising

content as AI servers and networking switches require more advanced materials; and 3)
potential M9 and M10 penetration in AI GPU and ASIC platforms. We expect stronger
pricing, improving utilization and a richer product mix to support a 38% sales CAGR
from 2026-2028E. However, general-server exposure (over 50% of sales) adds demand
uncertainty despite solid specification upgrades, as customers prioritize AI investments.
We therefore apply a target P/E at the mid-to-low end of its historical range, reflecting

broader tech-sector selling and rising concerns over slower AI spending. At 6x/5x
2027E/2028E VA consensus P/E, the stock looks attractive, with further estimate upside
if M9+ penetration in AI GPU and ASIC platforms exceeds expectations.
CCL upcycle supported by supply tightness and improving utilization
Strong server and AI demand, alongside fiberglass shortages and tight high-end copper-
foil supply, is supporting CCL pricing. ITEQ raised prices by c.30% in both Q226/Q326,

with further increases expected as pricing outpaces raw-material cost inflation.
Fiberglass shortages have limited utilization to 70-75%, but additional supply should
support recovery to at least 75-80% in '27E. Meanwhile, management guides capacity
expansion in Wuxi and Thailand will increase monthly capacity from current 5.4mn
sheets to 9.15mn by 2029F, supporting AI demand and geographic diversification.
General-server leadership and advanced CCL drive mix improvement

Intel Oak Stream and AMD Venice platform transitions should accelerate M7 adoption
as general servers migrate to PCIe Gen6 and higher motherboard layer counts.
Management expects M7 to reach 15-20% of sales by 2027F, versus high-single digits
today. With M7 priced at approximately 2x M6, this shift should lift ASPs and margins. AI
servers and networking switches offer further upside through more advanced materials,
with NVIDIA-qualified M9 potentially contributing to revenue from H127E. M9 carries

an ASP of 5-6x M6, although ramp timing remains uncertain. We forecast EPS of NT
$57.14/ NT$65.90 in 2027/2028E, up 141%/15% YoY.
Valuation: Initiate at Buy with a PE-based PT of NT$650
We initiate coverage of ITEQ with a Buy rating and PT of NT$650. Our estimates are
modestly below Street to reflect recent concerns on AI spending uncertainty thus
assuming capacity ramp 15-25% lower than company's announcements; but remain
constructive on structural growth outlook with potential upside from M9+ opportunities

in AI GPU and custom ASIC platforms. ITEQ trades at approximately 6x/5x our
2027E/2028E EPS post recent pullback, at trough cycle multiple, and  see attractive risk-
reward even on  conservative estimates.

ITEQ Corporation UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can penetration into upstream AI-grade M9 CCL materials drive next leg of growth?
Likely. Riding on the AI infrastructure supercycle, while as a latecomer, ITEQ is prioritizing high-end
CCL materials (M7+ CCL materials were high single digit % of H126 sales) and has received
qualification for its M9 low CTE materials for a major US AI GPU chipset supplier in late 2025. We

believe it might start to supply M9 materials for non-MGX platforms late 2026 or into 2027, with
likely low single digit % of total sales in 2027E. If ITEQ were able to secure further AI GPU or ASIC
projects, we see meaningful revenue streams and margin potential being unlocked, boosted by
significantly higher value for M9.
Q: Are the mid-grade CCL material opportunities underappreciated by the market?
Yes, we believe the market has underestimated ITEQ's growth and duration of conventional CCL

upcycle. ITEQ has been strategically focusing on general server and networking/switch applications,
where platform migrations and higher communication/computing requirements are leading to
increasing layer counts and higher-grade CCL materials that should drive favourable product mix shift
and structurally stronger pricing power over the long run, in our view. Near-term, we see an AI-driven
supply squeeze on both conventional CCL and upstream e-glass fabric capacity as incumbents

reallocate toward higher-margin AI-grade CCL and specialty glass fabric. Mid to long-term, we see
steady revenue growth with improving margin resilience on higher content value and higher asks for
vertical integration across key ingredients.
We believe ITEQ is well positioned to benefit from the AI infrastructure buildout due to its: 1) leading
position in general-server CCL and exposure to M7 adoption; 2) rising content as AI servers and
switches require more advanced materials; and 3) potential M9 and M10 penetration in AI GPU and

ASIC platforms. However, general-server exposure (50%+ of sales) adds demand uncertainty despite
specification upgrades, as customers prioritize AI investments.
CCL suppliers raised prices by 10-30% QoQ in Q126 and Q226, followed by further double-digit
increases in August and September. UBS expects CCL price increases through Q426E and into H127E
as raw material supply tightness continues. ITEQ has received qualification for its M9 low CTE
materials for a major US AI GPU chipset supplier at late 2025, which should further support pricing

uplift and enhance margin profile. ITEQ's gross margin has improved to 34% in Q326 from 14% in
2025 thanks to better product mix and favourable pricing environment on material shortages.
ITEQ’s share price has fallen 58% from its October peak, versus a 26% decline in the TAIEX over the
same period. The stock now trades at 6x/5x 2027E/2028E VA consensus P/E, with its NTM P/E one
standard deviation below its historical average. We value ITEQ at 10x 2028E P/E, toward the mid-to-

low end of its historical range, implying a 0.3x PEG against our forecast 29% earnings CAGR over
2026-2028E. We see current valuations as attractive, with further upside to our estimates if high-end
CCL materials opportunities materialize faster or at greater scale than expected.

Company Description

### Page 3

ITEQ Corporation UBS Research
Business Overview
Founded in 1997 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, ITEQ Corporation is a specialty
electronic-materials supplier focused primarily on copper-clad laminate (CCL) and
prepreg (PP). The company initially served a broad range of consumer electronics and
communications applications before progressively shifting its portfolio toward data-
center infrastructure. Between 2018 and 2025, consumer electronics declined from

43% to approximately 10% of revenue, while infrastructure increased from 37% to
68%. This transition was underpinned by ITEQ’s established position in general-purpose
servers. The company held an estimated 55% share of the general-server CCL market in
2017 and became a major laminate supplier for Intel’s Purley server platform.

ITEQ product mix (H126)
Source: Company data
ITEQ has four main business segments by application: Infrastructure, Automotive,

Consumer Electronics and Smartphone.
1. Infrastructure (73% of H126 revenue):
 General Server (~80% of Infrastructure): The traditional server market
remains the largest portion of infrastructure deployments, supporting
enterprise workloads, cloud computing, storage, and data center operations.
These platforms typically utilize standard multilayer PCBs and mainstream CCL
materials with relatively stable technology requirements.
 AI Applications (~15% of Infrastructure): AI applications include AI servers,

GPU/XPU accelerator boards, training clusters, and inference platforms. ITEQ
supplies high-speed, low-loss, and ultra-low-loss CCL materials used in high-
layer-count PCBs for AI computing and accelerator modules.
 Networking Switch (~5% of Infrastructure): Networking applications
include Ethernet switches, routers, and AI cluster interconnect equipment.
ITEQ supplies high-speed and high-frequency CCL materials used in switch and
networking PCBs supporting 400G, 800G, and next-generation data

transmission standards.
2. Automotive (14% of H126 revenue): Automotive applications include electric
vehicles (EVs), battery management systems (BMS), internet-of-vehicles (IoV)
communication modules, ADAS, radar modules, antenna modules, and
autonomous driving platforms utilizing high-performance computing (HPC) and
graphics processors. ITEQ supplies high-Tg, HDI, high-speed, and high-frequency
laminate materials used in the PCBs supporting these systems.

3. Consumer Electronics (8% of H126 revenue): Consumer electronics
applications include notebooks, tablets, gaming devices, wearables, and
household electronics. ITEQ supplies HDI, ultra-thin, and multilayer laminate
materials used in the main logic boards, connectivity modules, and power
management circuits of these devices.
4. Smartphone (5% of H126 revenue): Smartphone applications include RF
modules, antenna modules, HDI mainboards, camera modules, and 5G

communication platforms. ITEQ supplies HDI, high-frequency, and low-loss
laminate materials used in the high-density PCBs required for mobile connectivity
and signal transmission.

### Page 4

Well positioned for general server refresh and AI build out with 73% of
Infrastructure exposure in H126
General-purpose servers remain ITEQ's largest end market, accounting for
approximately 58% of total revenue and roughly 80% of infrastructure sales. The
company maintains deep customer relationships across the server supply chain,
including leading server ODMs and PCB manufacturers, and has participated in major
server platforms since Intel's Purley and AMD's Zen generations launched in 2017.

Historically, the business has benefited from a server refresh cycle of approximately four
to five years, with each new CPU platform driving higher PCB complexity and material
content. Notably, server motherboard layer counts typically increase by around four
layers with each major CPU generation upgrade, reflecting growing requirements for
power delivery, memory channels, and high-speed connectivity. Looking ahead, the
transition to Intel Oak Stream and AMD Venice platforms in 2026-27 is expected to drive

the next upgrade cycle centered on PCIe Gen6. The higher signaling speeds associated
with PCIe Gen6 require lower-loss PCB materials, accelerating the industry's migration
toward higher-performance laminates. As a result, next-generation server platforms are
expected to increasingly adopt M7 materials, compared with the heavier use of M6 in
current designs. Management expects M7 to account for approximately 15-20% of
sales by 2027F, up from a high-single-digit percentage today. Given that M7 carries

roughly twice the selling price of M6 and offers superior margins, the mix shift toward
higher-end materials should provide a meaningful uplift to revenue and profitability.
Beyond the platform transition, the long-term demand outlook remains favourable. As
the UBS global tech team highlighted, bottom-up server CPU volume could increase
from 23m units in 2025 to 67m units in 2030E, with x86 CPU shipments (Intel, AMD)
rising from 19m in 2025 to 40m in 2030E while ARM-based CPUs grow from 4m to

27m, reflecting rising adoption in hyperscale and AI-driven workloads. Servers made up
~58% of ITEQ's Q226 sales, with strong exposure to general server and a stable share,
we forecast a 31% 2026-30 CAGR for server revenue, reaching 70% of total sales in
2030E.
Figure 3: Beneficiary of increasing layer count on CPU upgrade cycles
Source: Company data, UBS research estimates
AI applications contribute approximately 11% of ITEQ's H126 revenue, or around 15%

of infrastructure sales, and represent the company's highest-value content opportunity.
Compared with traditional servers, AI platforms require more complex PCB architectures
featuring higher layer counts, larger board sizes, faster SerDes connectivity, and
significantly greater networking density, driving increased consumption of advanced
laminates. Material requirements are also migrating up the technology stack, with
growing adoption of M7, M8, and M9 grades, alongside ultra-thin dielectric materials,

low-Dk/low-Df laminates for signal integrity, and low-CTE materials to manage thermal
stress. ITEQ qualified an M9 product with NVIDIA in H225F, with revenue contribution
potentially beginning in H127E, although the timing and ramp remain uncertain. While
the company currently trails EMC in NVIDIA-related high-end CCL and is likely to enter
as a secondary supplier, the economics remain attractive, as M9 carries an ASP

### Page 5

approximately 5-6x that of M6 and gross margins exceeding 50%, versus roughly 25-
30% for M6 and over 30% for M7. As a result, even a modest penetration of M9
products could provide a meaningful uplift to ITEQ's blended margin profile. We
therefore expect gross margin to improve from 14% in 2025 to 38.5% in 2028E.
Networking switches account for 4% of ITEQ's H126 revenue, equivalent to around 5%
of infrastructure sales. As the connectivity backbone of AI clusters and cloud data

centers, switches route traffic between servers, accelerators, storage systems, and other
network nodes. The transition from 400G and 800G Ethernet toward 1.6T is driving a
rapid increase in PCB complexity, with leading-edge switch platforms often requiring
30+ layer and, in some cases, 40+ layer PCBs, alongside stricter signal-integrity
requirements and greater use of low-loss and ultra-low-loss laminate materials. ITEQ has
relevant experience in communications infrastructure and was previously a leading

Taiwanese CCL supplier for 5G base stations. While its position in high-end switching is
less established than in general servers, where TUC has traditionally been strong in
complex networking platforms and EMC has gained share in AI-related deployments,
switches remain an attractive adjacent market given their higher advanced-material
content and greater adoption of M7 and other premium laminate grades. Further
market-share gains would diversify ITEQ's infrastructure exposure beyond general

servers while increasing its mix of higher-value products.

Automotive provides diversification with exposure to secular growth trends,
accounting for 14% of revenue in H126
Automotive provides ITEQ exposure to several long-term vehicle electronics trends,

including electric vehicles (EVs), internet-of-vehicles (IoV) connectivity, active safety
systems, and autonomous driving applications. Key end markets include battery
management systems (BMS), infotainment and telematics modules, smart cockpits,
ADAS platforms, radar and antenna modules, as well as autonomous driving systems
utilizing high-performance computing (HPC) processors. In response to vehicle
electrification and intelligentization trends, ITEQ has mass-produced highly reliable

laminates featuring conductive anodic filament (CAF) resistance and high glass
transition temperature (High Tg) characteristics, supporting demanding automotive
applications such as power batteries, ADAS, and intelligent cockpit systems. The
company's automotive portfolio also includes high-speed and high-frequency materials
designed to meet stringent reliability, thermal, and signal-integrity requirements. As
vehicles become increasingly software-defined, rising PCB complexity, higher data

transmission speeds, and greater computing requirements continue to increase CCL
content per vehicle. Importantly, automotive programs typically remain in production for
5-7 years following qualification, providing greater revenue visibility and customer
stickiness than most consumer electronics applications. The segment also serves as a
stable outlet for ITEQ's M4-M6 product portfolio, diversifies the company's revenue
base away from AI-related capital expenditure cycles, and provides a durable long-term

growth avenue. However, we still expect infrastructure to grow faster than automotive,
reducing the segment’s share of total revenue from 15.9% in 2025 to 12.8% in 2030E.

### Page 6

Figure 6: Advanced automotive applications
Source: Company data
Consumer Electronics remains strategically valuable despite only 8% of sales
Consumer electronics include notebooks, tablets, and other computing devices. While
portions of the market are relatively commoditized, premium notebook platforms
increasingly require HDI and higher-layer-count PCB materials, supporting demand for
higher-value laminates. Although end-market growth is structurally lower than

infrastructure and AI applications, relatively short product refresh cycles of 1-2 years
continue to generate recurring replacement demand. The segment also remains
strategically important given ITEQ's longstanding relationships with major Taiwanese
ODMs such as Quanta, Wistron, and Inventec, many of which are also key participants in
the server and AI supply chains. As a result, consumer electronics serves not only as a
stable revenue contributor but also as a channel through which ITEQ maintains and

expands customer relationships across higher-growth infrastructure markets. While
consumer electronics remains strategically important, we expect its share of total
revenue to decline from 10.5% in 2025 to 3.3% in 2030E as growth remains
concentrated in infrastructure.
Smartphones contribute 5% of sales but are unlikely to be a key growth driver
Smartphones remain ITEQ's smallest disclosed end market at approximately 5% of
revenue. The company has developed ultra-thin dielectric materials and resin-coated

copper for compact handheld devices, but it has historically trailed EMC in high-end HDI
applications. Smartphone PCBs require thin dielectric layers, fine wiring, high-density
interconnection, and excellent dimensional stability. These are technically demanding
products, but the market is mature and concentrated among a limited number of major
brands and PCB manufacturers. ITEQ's smartphone exposure appears to be weighted
more heavily toward Chinese Tier 2 handset makers and their associated supply chains,

while EMC maintains a stronger position in premium flagship platforms. As a result, the
segment offers less attractive growth and margin potential than infrastructure, where
ITEQ enjoys a stronger competitive position. We therefore expect smartphones to
remain a relatively small part of the portfolio, with management likely to participate
selectively to support technology development, customer diversification, and capacity
utilization.
Capacity expansion drives both volume growth and product mix upgrade from

LEO and AI
Based on the company's guidance,  ITEQ's laminate capacity is expected to increase from
approximately 5.4mn sheets per month currently to 9.15mn sheets by 2029F. This
includes expansion at its Thailand facility, where the remaining 300k sheets of Phase 1
capacity are scheduled to come online in Q426, followed by an additional 600k sheets
expected in 2028. While the Thailand operation was historically a relatively small
contributor, management expects it to begin mass production for a major low-earth-

orbit (LEO) satellite program in 2027, initially focused on M4 and M6 products with
limited M7 content. This facility provides geographic diversification and helps serve Tier-
1 US customers seeking manufacturing capacity outside Taiwan and Mainland China.
In addition, ITEQ is expanding capacity in Wuxi, which is expected to add approximately
2.85mn sheets per month by 2029F, including 600k sheets in Q427F and a further

### Page 7

1.35mn  sheets in Q428F. Unlike Thailand, the Wuxi expansion is primarily targeted at AI
and high-performance computing demand, where customers increasingly require
premium laminate grades. ITEQ has already qualified its M9 material with NVIDIA and
has begun sampling M9 and M10 products to Google. Successful qualification would
expand the company's participation in AI GPUs, custom ASICs, high-end networking
switches, and next-generation accelerator platforms. Given the significantly higher ASPs

and margins associated with M7-M10 materials, the Wuxi expansion should not only
increase capacity but also improve the company's product mix, further increasing its
exposure to AI infrastructure spending.

Equipment remains the key industry bottleneck, while fiberglass constrains
near-term utilization
The principal constraint on industry CCL capacity expansion is equipment availability
rather than raw materials. Lead times for Treater Lines (Impregnation Lines) have
extended from roughly 6 months to 12-18 months, while Lamination Press lead times

have increased from 6 to 9 months, implying that much of the capacity currently being
planned may not come online until 2027 or 2028. By contrast, raw material lead times
are generally much shorter, typically around 2 months. One notable characteristic of the
CCL industry is that capacity is relatively fungible, allowing manufacturers to adjust
product mix and utilization rates quickly in response to demand. As a result, once
equipment is delivered, installation and ramp-up can be completed relatively quickly,

making equipment lead times the primary constraint on industry-wide capacity
additions.
At the company level, however, fiberglass cloth is currently ITEQ's near-term production
bottleneck. The company sources material from Nittobo, Asahi, and Taiwan Glass, while
also qualifying Chinese suppliers including Taishan, Jushi, and Honghe. Tightness is not
limited to premium fiberglass grades, as manufacturers have increasingly converted
capacity toward low-Dk1 and low-Dk2 products, reducing the availability of

conventional E-glass. Management expects additional Chinese supply to become
available in Q426F, supporting a utilization increase from 70-75% to 75-80% in H127,
with further industry additions potentially supporting higher utilization.
Copper foil is less constrained today but could emerge as the next bottleneck as AI-
related demand ramps. While conventional and battery-grade copper foil remain
oversupplied, supply of advanced HVLP4 and HVLP5 foil is considerably tighter due to

the technical challenges associated with producing ultra-smooth foil for high-speed PCB
applications. These materials are increasingly required for M9 and M10 laminates, where
minimizing signal loss is critical. Although Taiwanese and Chinese producers are
expanding capacity, qualification requirements and yield ramp challenges may limit
near-term supply growth. As a result, while equipment remains the key limiter of
industry capacity expansion, fiberglass availability is the most immediate operational

constraint for ITEQ, with high-end copper foil emerging as a potential bottleneck for
next-generation AI materials from late 2026 onward.

### Page 8

ITEQ Corporation UBS Research
Financial analysis
CCL upcycle drives strong H126 earnings
ITEQ’s H126 sales reached NT$20.7bn, up 24% HoH, primarily driven by the CCL
upcycle amid raw-material shortages, while infrastructure revenue grew 29% HoH.
Gross margin expanded 5.6ppts HoH to 33.4%, reflecting a favorable product-mix shift,
higher contributions from higher-margin infrastructure sales, and price hikes. Operating
expenses increased by NT$63mn HoH to NT$1.3bn, driven by higher selling and

administrative spending to support growth initiatives; however, opex as a percentage of
sales declined 2.3ppts HoH to 12.8%, reflecting improved operating leverage.
Operating profit increased 112% HoH, more than offsetting the decline in non-
operating income to NT$45mn from NT$89mn in H225. As a result, H126 EPS surged
112% HoH to NT$4.39.
Q326 EPS of NT$8.41 beat consensus of NT$5.40 on stronger sales and GM. Sales rose
35% QoQ and 102% YoY to NT$15.6bn, while GM expanded 12.3ppts QoQ to 34.3%,

benefiting from price hikes and an improved product mix. OpM also expanded 11.8ppts
QoQ to 27.8%, slightly less than GM, due to a NT$335mn increase in opex to NT$1bn,
mainly on employee bonuses. Opex should remain stable at NT$0.9–1bn going forward.
By application, infrastructure accounted for 74% of sales, up 1ppt QoQ, while
automotive also increased 1ppt QoQ to 15%. Consumer electronics and smartphones
each declined 1ppt QoQ to 7% and 4%, respectively.

Q426 to see continued pricing momentum
We forecast Q426E sales of NT$18.6bn, up 19% QoQ, supported by an additional 300k
sheets of capacity from Thailand Phase I. We expect GM to remain strong at 36.5%, as
management expects double-digit product price increases to take effect in Q4,
reflecting higher raw-material costs amid continued E-glass cloth tightness. Further
tightening in copper foil and low-DK supply into 2027 could sustain pricing momentum.

Management expects general-server demand to remain strong and the M6-to-M7
transition to continue into 2027E. We forecast Q426E EPS of NT$10.93, up 30% QoQ.

Advanced CCL mix drives revenue and margin expansion through 2028E
We forecast ITEQ’s revenue to grow 66%/67%/13% YoY in 2026/2027/2028E, driven
by the Intel Oak Stream and AMD Venice server refresh cycles, rising PCIe Gen6

adoption, increasing AI-related demand, and new capacity in Thailand and Wuxi.
General servers should remain the largest revenue contributor, with higher motherboard
layer counts and the transition from M6 to M7 materials supporting greater CCL

### Page 9

content, higher ASPs, and improved profitability. In AI applications, we expect growth to
be driven by increasing adoption of M7-M9 materials, with M9 potentially accounting
for 1% of sales in 2028E, supported by an expected revenue contribution from NVIDIA-
qualified M9 product starting in H127E. Given that M9 carries substantially higher ASPs
and gross margins than mainstream products, even modest penetration could
meaningfully enhance ITEQ’s revenue mix and blended profitability, although the timing

and scale of the ramp remain uncertain. Networking switches should provide an
additional growth avenue as the transition from 400G and 800G toward 1.6T drives
higher layer counts and greater demand for advanced low-loss materials, while the
Thailand facility’s planned mass production for a major LEO satellite program should
further diversify the revenue base.
We forecast gross margin to increase from 14% in 2025 to 29%/38%/38% in
2026/2027/2028E, supported by price hikes, higher utilization, operating scale, and a

richer mix of M7-M9 products, partly offset by potential fiberglass and advanced
copper-foil constraints, qualification uncertainty, and initial costs associated with new
capacity ramps. We expect operating margin to improve from 7% in 2025 to
22%/32%/33% in 2026/2027/2028E, as gross margin expansion and operating
leverage more than offset higher selling, administrative, and R&D expenses associated
with capacity expansion and advanced-material development. As a result, we forecast

EPS of NT$57.14/NT$65.90 in 2027/2028E, representing growth of 141%/15% YoY,
respectively.

### Page 10

Sensitivity analysis
Quantifying earnings downside  risks if AI spending slows down from 2028
The US Department of Commerce measures announced on 16 November seek to
prevent China AI-chip controls from being circumvented through third-country data
centers. The rules extend controls beyond the physical shipment of advanced chips to
who ultimately accesses the compute, while introducing licensing requirements for
advanced-chip exports, re-exports and transfers to Singapore, Malaysia, Indonesia and

Thailand, as well as Brazil, which BIS identifies as presenting higher-than-normal
diversion risk. Chips destined for US-headquartered companies or US-parented
subsidiaries are exempt from this country-level requirement, subject to reporting
conditions. The rules do not altogether prohibit ASEAN AI investment, but raise licensing
and compliance hurdles for parts of the data center ecosystem (LINK). This raises
concerns on first-order impacts of likely to be delayed GPU deployment and slower data

center build-outs. While we believe AI data center build-out remains supported by  cloud
computing power growth, enterprise AI and inference, we note it is constrained by data-
sovereignty and latency requirements, as well as power and land supply limits. We
believe the market continues to expect a strong 2027 but is now more cautious for
2028. We think some market expectations of a down year might be too conservative as
the UBS team has been noting that operating cash flow should support sustainable

capex for 2028. That being said, we still see some sensitivity around potential acts might
cap hyperscalers on securing permitted sites and/or receiving power/water permits.
Below we examine the potential downside risks into 2028 earnings if AI spending
materially slows down vs our current base case scenario. Our key assumptions and
findings are as follows:
 If capacity expansion plan fall short of expectations... The company has
announced plans to expand CCL monthly capacity from 5.35mn sheets in 2025 to

8.25mn/9.15mn sheets by 2028F/2030F. As we take a more conservative stance to
factor in the AI demand uncertainties, our current model assumes its CCL monthly
capacity at 6.55mn sheets in 2028E, below the company's announced plan of
8.25mn sheets; and note that if capacity additions were only
50%/30%/20%/10% executed compared to our base case scenario, this would
imply potential 2028E earnings downside of 25%/33%/38%/42% to our current
estimates.

 ...and pricing environment becomes less favourable: We currently expect to
see AI CCL content value increases driven by computing and networking/
interconnect demands; and extended equipment lead time and qualification
process along with increasing manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more
constrained upstream inputs. That being said, if pricing power starts to weaken on
AI spending slowdown, assuming pricing falls short of our current estimates by

5%/10%/15%/20%, on top of lowered capacity, this would imply potential
earnings downside of 31%/45%/54%/61% to our current 2028E estimates.

ITEQ Corporation UBS Research
Business overview
ITEQ, founded in 1997 and headquartered in Taiwan, is a manufacturer of copper clad
laminate (CCL), prepreg (PP), flexible CCL, and specialty laminate materials used in
printed circuit boards (PCB), serving a broad range of end markets including automotive,
smartphone, consumer electronics, and infrastructure. The company has built a
competitive position through its expertise in advanced resin formulation and process

engineering, supplying major PCB manufacturers. Today, ITEQ focuses on developing
next-generation material solutions for AI, HPC, automotive, and LEO.

Chairperson and President of WIN Semiconductors and holds various board

of ITEQ's outstanding shares as of November 20, 2026.
 CEO and General Manager - Ms. Audrey (Hsin-Hui) Tsai: Ms. Tsai serves as
CEO, General Manager, and Board Director of ITEQ. She has been a board

member since 2015 and is responsible for overseeing the company's global
operations, business strategy, and technology roadmap. Ms. Tsai holds a
Master's degree in Finance (EMBA) from National Taiwan University and a
Bachelor's degree in Finance and Taxation from National Chengchi University.
Ms. Tsai held 0.3% of ITEQ's outstanding shares as of March 29, 2026.
Shareholder structure
Publicly listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange, ITEQ has a concentrated shareholder

structure led by strategic shareholder WIN Semiconductors, which owned 18.0% of the
company as of November 20, 2026. Other major shareholders include Tianhe Xingye
Co., Ltd. (11.8%), Winwin Venture Capital (2.4%), and Winsun Investment (2.2%),
while Chairman Dennis (Chin-Tsai) Chen personally held approximately 1.7% of
outstanding shares. Foreign institutional participation has increased alongside the
company's growing exposure to AI server, networking, and high-speed computing

infrastructure markets, with global investors such as Vanguard, Dimensional Fund,
BlackRock, and Nomura appearing among the top shareholders. ITEQ's shareholder
base reflects a combination of strategic ownership through the WIN Group, long-term
domestic investors, and institutional investors.

Source: Refinitiv Eikon
ITEQ’s P/E valuation has historically been cyclical, with its 12-month forward P/E reaching

troughs of 7x in October 2018 and July 2022, versus a historical average of 14x. The
stock re-rated from H225, driven by: 1) the CCL upcycle amid tightening raw-material
supply and industry price increases; 2) expectations for a general-server refresh cycle led
by Intel Oak Stream and AMD Venice; and 3) growing confidence in ITEQ’s transition
from mainstream CCL toward higher-value AI-grade materials. Its 12-month forward P/E
subsequently reached a historical peak of 36x in April 2026.

Following a 58% share-price decline from its October 2026 peak, versus a 26% decline
in the TAIEX over the same period, we believe the valuation now offers an attractive
entry point. ITEQ trades at consensus P/E multiples of 6x/5x for 2027E/2028E, with its
NTM P/E one standard deviation below its historical average. While our estimates are
modestly below Street, we are constructive on earnings growth and see longer-term
upside from high-end AI-server CCL materials, particularly M9 and above.

We expect continued specification upgrades, increasing M7 adoption, improving
utilization and a richer product mix to support earnings expansion. Potential upside
drivers include: 1) increasing M7 adoption as Intel Oak Stream and AMD Venice migrate
to PCIe Gen6, supporting higher ASPs and margins; 2) revenue contribution from ITEQ’s
NVIDIA-qualified M9 product from H127E; and 3) additional M9 and M10 qualification
wins across AI GPU, custom ASIC and high-end networking-switch applications. Our

Buy thesis does not require these opportunities to exceed expectations, but faster or
larger-scale commercialization could provide further upside to our estimates.
Nevertheless, ITEQ remains more heavily geared toward general servers, which account
for 50+% of sales. Despite continued specification upgrades, demand carries greater
uncertainty as customers prioritize AI investments. The timing and scale of M9+ revenue
contributions also remain uncertain, while margin sustainability will depend partly on

product-mix improvement as raw-material tightness and CCL pricing normalize. These
risks warrant a conservative valuation multiple, rather than a return to the premium
multiples seen at the cycle peak.
We value ITEQ at 10x 2028E P/E, towards the mid-to-low end of its historical range,
implying a 0.3x PEG against our forecast 29% earnings CAGR over 2026-2028E. Our
conservative multiple reflects broader tech-sector selling, rising concerns over a
potential slowdown in AI spending and ITEQ’s current general-server gearing. While

EMC and TUC remain the most direct CCL comparables, we see scope for ITEQ’s
valuation to strengthen as meaningful M9+ revenue and broader participation in
leading AI platforms demonstrate a more structurally supported earnings and margin
profile. Our target multiple compares with 12x/8x for EMC/TUC. We believe the discount
ITEQ is trading at NT$355.50 (as of 20
November 2026)

### Page 15

to EMC appropriately reflects ITEQ’s product positioning, while its improving earnings
profile supports a premium to the broader CCL and PCB groups, which trade at 8x and
7x 2028E P/E on average, respectively. At current valuations, we see attractive risk-
reward and initiate coverage at Buy with a price target of NT$650, with further upside to
our estimates if high-end CCL materials opportunities materialize faster or at greater
scale than expected.

74%/25%, while GMs expand from 38%/38% to 41%/43%, reflecting stronger-than-
expected AI server demand, faster penetration of high-speed CCL materials, and a richer

high-speed CCL materials, and a richer high-end product mix. We then apply ~10x 2028E
EPS to arrive at our NT$650 base case valuation.
DOWNSIDE (NT$235): Our downside case lowers 2027-28E sales growth from

general server demand, slower penetration of high-speed CCL materials, and a weaker-
than-expected high-end product mix. We then apply ~7x 2028E EPS to arrive at our NT

$235 downside valuation.

### Page 17

ITEQ Corporation UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
ITEQ is a leading global supplier of copper clad laminate (CCL),
prepreg (PP), flexible CCL and specialty materials used in printed
circuit boards (PCBs). Founded in 1997, ITEQ initially served
consumer electronics and communications applications, before
expanding into server, data center and automotive materials. It
has since built relationships with leading PCB manufacturers and

server supply-chain customers. In 2025, infrastructure accounted
for 68% of sales, followed by automotive at 16%, consumer
electronics at 10% and smartphones at 5%.
Industry Outlook
The CCL industry outlook remains positive, supported by AI
server investment and upgrades to high-speed networking
infrastructure. Larger, higher-layer-count PCBs and tighter signal-
integrity requirements are increasing demand for high-
performance, ultra-low-loss laminates, supporting both material

content growth and a richer product mix. Tight supply of
specialty glass fabric and other inputs is supporting laminate
price increases, although rising material costs and supply
constraints remain risks to margins and shipment growth. These
trends should favour suppliers with advanced material
capabilities and reliable access to critical inputs.

CCL, 72%

Company Description
ITEQ is a leading global supplier of copper clad laminate (CCL), prepreg (PP), flexible CCL and
specialty materials used in printed circuit boards (PCBs). Founded in 1997, ITEQ initially served
consumer electronics and communications applications, before expanding into server, data

center and automotive materials. It has since built relationships with leading PCB
manufacturers and server supply-chain customers. In 2025, infrastructure accounted for 68%
of sales, followed by automotive at 16%, consumer electronics at 10% and smartphones at
5%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value ITEQ using a PE-based methodology
Industry risks: Demand for CCL remains dependent on data-centre investment, server
demand, and broader electronics industry conditions. Slower-than-expected AI infrastructure

deployments, delays in server upgrade cycles, or weaker IT spending could reduce demand
for CCL and weigh on industry revenue growth and margins. In addition, increasing
competition and capacity expansion could lead to pricing pressure and lower industry
profitability.
Company-specific risks: ITEQ faces execution risk as it shifts toward higher-value CCL
products. The company could experience weaker-than-expected growth if new products or
capacity ramp more slowly than planned. It could also face margin pressure from raw-

material shortages, higher input costs, or an unfavourable product mix.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Elite Material
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$7,400, based on a target PE multiple of 28x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our
target PE multiple, at the peak of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings outlook supported
by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC (high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec

upgrade for CCL, price hikes and product mix enhancement should help support its margin expansion going forward. At our
target price, the shares would trade at 55x/28x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which we largely attribute to frequent debates over the
spec upgrade for next-gen AI servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope for aggressive spec
upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in

the end. However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth
potential among AI servers of different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL
upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg, glass, copper foil,

foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

陳玫芬 合格證券投資分析人員 & CFP 
Lisa.mf.chen@yuanta.com 
陳亮齊 
chrischenlc@yuanta.com

InP 基板缺貨影響光通訊，3Q26 營運低於預期且 4Q 估計下修 
3Q26 營收 3.2 億元，季增 25%且年增 27%；毛利率/營益率為 39%/18%，
季增 4.7 ppts/9.7 ppts，EPS 1.26 元，季增 214%/年增 68%，低於預期 23%；
光通訊占比為 25%，低於 1Q26/2Q26 的 45%/37%。光通訊比重低使毛利率
較原估計低 3.9 ppts。估計 4Q26 營收 3.77 億元，季增 18% (10 月營收已達
1.24 億元)，下修 14%，主因 InP 基板持續缺貨中；毛利率下修 4.4ppts 至
38.8%；EPS 估計為 1.31 元，下修 38%。 
GaAs 應用之研發案增加，包含 VCSEL、QD Laser 及 PIN 
IET-KY 的 MBE 磊晶包含 GaAs(砷化鎵)、InP(磷化銦)、GaSb(銻化鎵)等材
料，雖然 InP 現階段基板不足，惟 GaAs 的研發案近期有所斬獲，如(1) 射頻
用於國防；(2) VCSEL，200G 射頻用於光通訊(市場尚未有量產者)；(3) 
QD(量子點)雷射，發射端已於 2026 年中送樣再調整結構中；(4) PIN，用於
光通訊的接收端。此外，GaSb(銻化鎵)估計 2026 年增 65%且占比 19%以國
防應用為主，近期亦有新的商業需求正與客戶洽談中。 
2026 及 2027 年 EPS 估計下修 31%及 29%，目前評價合理 
IET-KY 目前光通訊出貨以 100G 及 200G 的 PD 為主；公司無採用上游中國
大陸設廠之 AXT 的 InP 基板，目前採日系基板為主且今年處於即到貨即用的
緊俏狀態。德系供應商期待後續產能提升但仍需時間，本中心估計 2026 年
光通訊占比由 2025 年的 44%降至 35%，2027 年回到占比 44%。本中心下
修估計 2026/2027 年營收 13%/13%至 12.85 億/18.61 億元，年增 19%/45%，
毛利率 40%/43%，下修 3.5/4.7 百分點，EPS 為 4.63 元/9.03 元，年增
184%/95%，下修 31%/29%。因 InP 基板取得不確定性而下修目標本益比，
採估計 2027 年 EPS 以 45 倍目標本益比(前次採 57 倍)給予目標價 410 元，
目前評價合理給予持有之投資評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
InP 基板缺貨影響使 3Q26 獲利低於預期 23%至 EPS 1.26 元，且光通
訊占比為 25%，低於 1Q26/2Q26 的 45%/37%；4Q 估 EPS 1.31 元。 
⧫  
GaAs 應用之研發案增加，包含 VCSEL、QD Laser 及 PIN，有機會於
2028 年貢獻；此外，GaSb 於國防及商業需求均提升。 
⧫  
採日系基板為主且處於即到貨即用的緊俏狀態；2026 及 2027 年 EPS
估計下修 31%及 29%至 4.6 及 9.0 元，呈高度成長惟目前評價合理。

3Q26 EPS 1.26 元低於預期 23%，InP 基板缺貨使毛利率低於原估計 
3Q26 營收 3.2 億元，季增 25%且年增 27%；毛利率/營益率為 39%/18%，季增 4.7 ppts/9.7ppts，
EPS 1.26 元，季增 214%且年增 68%，低於預期 23%；光通訊占比為 25%，低於 1Q26/2Q26 的
45%/37%。光通訊比重低使毛利率較原估計低 3.9 ppts。估計 4Q26 營收 3.8 億元，季增 18% (10
月營收已達 1.24 億元)，下修 14%；毛利率下修 4.4ppts 至 38.8%；EPS 估計為 1.31 元，下修 38%。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第3 頁，共 14頁
上游 InP 基板緊缺難以完全解決，惟公司本身可望於 2H27 稍改善
IET-KY 目前光通訊出貨以 100G 及 200G 的 PD 為主，InP 基板短缺影響營運；因公司若干產品用
於美國軍工國防，故無採用上游中國大陸設廠之 AXT 的 InP 基板，目前採日系基板比重達 80%，
而且今年處於即到貨即用的緊俏狀態。德系供應商期待後續產能提升，且部份客戶自行提供基板；
然而整體基板於市場供需仍然緊俏，其他磊晶廠商亦積極搶占基板料源，基板供應商均已規劃大幅
度擴充產能惟仍需時間，估計二年內均呈緊缺狀況，公司則於 2H27 稍微改善。
GaAs(砷化鎵)應用之研發案增加，GaSb(銻化鎵)亦有新的商業需求
IET-KY 的 MBE 磊晶包含 GaAs(砷化鎵)、InP(磷化銦)、GaSb(銻化鎵)等材料，雖然 InP 現階段基
板不足，惟 GaAs 的研發案近期有所斬獲，如(1)射頻用於國防；(2)VCSEL，200G 射頻用於光通訊

(市場尚未有量產者)；(3)QD(量子點)雷射，發射端已於 2026 年中送樣再調整結構中；(4)PIN，用
於光通訊的接收端。此外，GaSb(銻化鎵)估計 2026 年增 65%以國防應用為主，近期亦有新的商業
需求正與客戶洽談中。
光聖換股已完成，3Q26 於業外挹注股利收入 0.16 億元
IET-KY 與光聖換股已於 5 月完成，透過股份交換結盟後，雙方相互持股比例分別為光聖持有 IET-
KY 約 15.26% 普通股股權，IET-KY 持有光聖約 1.79% 普通股股權。3Q26 於業外挹注股利收入
0.16 億元，短期尚未有其他本業的助益，長期有機會與其子公司合作開發產品。
MBE 磊晶在厚度控制及品質具優勢，估 2026/2027 年營運明顯年增但較先前保守
IET-KY 營運主體位於美國德州，以 MBE(Molecular Beam Epitaxy；分子束磊晶)技術為核心，同時
具備機器設備開發能力。MBE 磊晶在厚度控制及品質上準確度高於 MOCVD，適合新品研發及高階

產品。高階光模組應用於 2026 年以 400G 及 800G 為主流，多採用 100G 的 PD。現階段 IET 出貨
以 100G PD 及 200G PD(可用於 200G 光模組)為主。公司 MBE 機台為 16 台，估 2027 年增至 19
台。由於 InP 基板缺貨難解，本中心下修 2026/2027 年營收 13%/13%至 12.85 億/18.61 億元，年
增 19%/45%，毛利率 40%/43%，下修 3.5/4.7 百分點，EPS 為 4.63 元/9.03 元，年增 184%/95%，
下修 31%/29%。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第4 頁，共 14頁  
圖 4：2025 年各季營收逐步走高，主要來自光通訊應用的貢獻

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理

圖 5：2025、2026F 及 2027F 年估計營收及營益率逐年走高

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估 
MBE 透過分子束提高均勻性、純度等，高階光通訊趨勢有利營運增長 
MBE (Molecular Beam Epitaxy)分子束磊晶製程為：(1)分子束形成：將坩鍋中的原料透過噴射源精
確加熱以昇華、蒸發分子形成分子束，分子如砷、鎵、磷、銦，噴嘴以石英克努森容器（Kundsen 
Cell）為主；(2)表面附著與擴散：分子束抵達加熱過的基板表面並吸附後將在晶格能量最低的地方
進行鍵結；(3)外延生長：透過精確控制基板溫度與分子束通量，薄膜會依照基板的晶體結構進行層
層堆疊最終形成磊晶。 
噴射源重要性在於透過熱管理、快門（Shutter）控制提供穩定、均勻的分子束，使薄膜品質穩定。
MBE 另外一大重要的設備為反射高能電子繞射儀 (Refection High-Energy Electron Diffraction, 
RHEED)，透過 RHEED 可以進行生長速率控制、生長狀態監控以即時控管磊晶品質。 
MBE 相較於 MOCVD 的優勢於：(1)精準度：透過分子束的控制更精準的製造且控制厚度；(2)減少
雜質：在原料端由於減去了前驅物的使用使材料純度更高，且基板遷移摻雜問題較小。然而，生長
速度較慢與且耗材要求較高，整體成本較高。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第5 頁，共 14頁  
產業概況  
光收發模組明顯成長且 800G 及 1.6T 零件更高階；NPO/CPO 數量低但高成長 
AI 對高速網路傳輸速率的需求持續攀升，光收發模組 (Optical Transceiver) 扮演關鍵角色；光收發
模組於 400G 以下且市場穩定；然而 400G 以上估年增 2026/2027 年為 80%/43%至 1 億/1.45 億個；
2026 年主流為 400G，2027 及 2028 年主流為 800G 且未來升級至 1.6T 以上則採更高階零組件，
如 Interposer、TEC 及 Submount 等電子級精密陶瓷。NPO/CPO 仍為初始階段，2026/2027/2028
年估 40 萬/320 萬/2,200 萬個：2027/2028 年增 680%/595%，惟數量遠低於光收發模組；CPO 將
OE 與交換機的 ASIC 共同封裝，降低傳輸功耗至 1/3；NPO 僅將 OE 與 ASIC 放置在交換機內同一
PCB 基板上，降低交換機傳輸功耗至 50%；二者均需插拔式 ELSFP 模組。 
圖 6：光收發模組(Optical Transceiver)市場預估數量成長；CPO/NPO 市場仍小但成長性高

插拔式光收發模組以光晶片、PCB 及 DSP 最短缺 
插拔式光收發模組以光晶片、PCB 及 DSP 最短缺；內部架構以 DSP 為主晶片，其與 TIA 及 Driver
組成 EIC；PIC 則包含 PD、 LD、調變器； OE(光引擎)包含 EIC 與 PIC，所有零件在傳輸速率提升
下持續升級。PCB 在高階光模組需 mSAP 製程具門檻， DSP 由國際少數廠商寡占使供需相當緊俏，
內部的發射及接收端光元件晶粒以國際大廠為主。 
圖 7：光收發模組零件供應商分工架構(左)；光晶片上下游供應商以大廠為主(右)

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第6 頁，共 14頁  
圖 8：光收發模組供應商、以插拔式光模組為主(左)，CPO/NPO 市場仍小但成長性高(右)

資料來源：網路資料、元大投顧整理

圖 9：光收發模組中的 OE (左)；CPO/NPO 將 OE 置交換機內與 ASIC 共同封裝/同一 PCB 板 (右)

資料來源：市場資料、元大投顧整理

資料傳輸更高速使光晶片數量及規格提升；CPO/NPO 亦需插拔式光模組 
AI 交換機(Switch) 單埠（Port）的傳輸資料量主要取決於交換機的世代技術，2026 年主流為單 Port 
800G 的傳輸速率採 8 通道且單埠每秒可傳輸約 100 GB 的資料；1.6T（1.6 Tbps）採 8 通道且單
埠每秒可傳輸達 200 GB 的資料；未來若發展 3.2T 將採 8 通道且單埠每秒可傳輸達 400 GB 的資
料。 
此外，CPO（Co-Packaged Optics；共同封裝光學）光子積體電路（PIC）與電子積體電路（EIC）
共同封裝在同一個載板上，目前市場的 CPO/NPO 之 AI 交換機(Switch)，採 ELSFP（External 
Laser Small Form Pluggable，外接雷射光源模組），調變晶片靠近 ASIC 位置以縮短傳輸距離及降
低功耗；ELSFP 將容易受温度影響且壽命較短的雷射光源獨立出來，放在主機板邊緣作為標準化
插拔模組，不僅提升散熱效率，也解決 CPO/NPO 系統的維護難題。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第7 頁，共 14頁
獲利調整與股票評價
MBE 磊晶法於高階應用具前景故提升機台產能，惟目前評價合理
IET-KY 為美國製造的 MBE(Molecular Beam Epitaxy；分子束磊晶)製程 III-V 族化合物磊晶廠，光
通訊(光纖網路)產品以應用於光收發模組為主要動能，占營收比重 2024 年的 29%升至 2025 年的
44%，帶動 2025 年毛利率年增 20 百分點至 36%。IET 的 100G 及 200G PD 可應用於 800G 及 1.6T
的光收發模組；未來市場朝 3.2T 邁進時需要 400G PD 且公司已進入研發階段，惟 2029 年後才有
顯著成長動能。
公司 MBE 機台為 16 台，估 2027 年增至 19 台。由於 InP 基板缺貨難解，本中心估計 2026 年光通
訊占比由 2025 年的 44%降至 35%，金額年減 5%；2027 年回到占比 44%，金額年增 79%。本中

心下修估計 2026/2027 年營收 13%/13%至 12.85 億/18.61 億元，年增 19%/45%，毛利率 40%/43%，
下修 3.5/4.7 百分點，EPS 為 4.63 元/9.03 元，年增 184%/95%，下修 31%/29%。因 InP 基板取得
不確定性而下修目標本益比，採估計 2027 年 EPS 以 45 倍目標本益比(前次採 57 倍)給予目標價
410 元，目前評價合理給予持有之投資評等。

公司簡介  
生產基地位於美國的Ⅲ-Ⅴ族化合物半導體 MBE 磊晶製造商，光通訊比重最高且持續攀升 
IET-KY 成立於 2011 年，營運主體位於美國德州，為Ⅲ-Ⅴ族化合物半導體磊晶片代工廠，以 MBE 磊晶技術為核心，產品
包含 GaAs(砷化鎵)、InP(磷化銦)，GaSb(銻化鎵)；應用領域包含無線通訊、衛星通訊、光纖通訊、夜視鏡、保全監控、太
空、軍事、生物醫學、資料傳輸、雲端試算等，光通訊 PD 磊晶為近期主要成長動能。

附錄：重要揭露事項 
分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明：(1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

IET-KY (4971 TT)- 投資建議與目標價三年歷史趨勢

# 日期 收盤價 (A) 目標價 (B) 調整後目標價 (C) 評等 分析師 
1 20260327 526.00 650 650 買進 陳玫芬 
2 20260508 750.00 855 855 持有-超越同業 陳玫芬

資料來源：CMoney、元大投顧 
註：A = 未調整之收盤價；B = 未調整之目標價； C = 依據股本變動調整後之目標價。員工分紅稀釋影響未反映於 A、B 或 C。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

### Page 1

기아 (000270/KS) 
라인업 확대의 성과와 주주환원에 주목 
2027 년 영업이익 7.96 조 원 전망 
2027 년 실적은 매출액 124.3 조 원(YoY +1.0%), 영업이익 7.96 조 원(YoY -
5.3%, OPM 6.4%), 지배주주순이익 6.97 조 원(YoY -0.2%)으로 전망한다. 우호적
이었던 환율 효과가 약해지는 가운데, 높아진 원재료 투입가격과 유럽을 중심으로 한 
판매 경쟁도 수익성에 부담으로 남는다. 판매를 지키기 위한 인센티브 확대까지 감안하
면 물량 증가가 그대로 이익 증가로 이어지기는 어렵다. 다만 이미 투입한 신차의 연간 
판매 기여와 미국, 유럽의 라인업 확대가 비용 부담을 일부 상쇄하면서, 하반기에는 전
년 동기 대비 증익으로 돌아설 것으로 본다.

지역별로 갖춘 제품, PBV 로 넓어지는 고객 
2027 년에는 이미 출시한 신차의 판매를 늘려 제품과 설비에 투자한 성과를 매출과 이
익으로 연결하는 일이 중요하다. 미국에서는 텔루라이드 등 주력 SUV 와 확대된 하이
브리드 차종으로 수요에 대응한다. 전기차 전환을 서두르지 않는 고객에게도 선택지를 
제공하며, 이 가운데 현지 생산 차종은 완성차 수입에 따른 관세 부담을 줄이는 데도 도
움이 된다. 유럽에서는 EV2·EV4 등 전기차와 신형 셀토스로 판매 기회를 넓힌다. 기
존 슬로바키아 공장을 활용해 전기차를 공급할 수 있지만, 중국 업체와의 경쟁 속에서 
판촉 부담을 관리하는 과제도 남아 있다.

PBV 는 PV5 에 이어 PV7 을 투입하며 물류 및 운송업체의 용도별 수요에 대응할 계
획이다. 차량 크기와 적재 수요에 맞춘 제품에 판매와 정비망을 더하면 고객과의 거래
를 넓힐 수 있다. 미국 하이브리드의 판매 확대, 유럽 전기차의 안착, PBV 의 고객 확보
는 각각 이익에 기여하는 속도가 다르다. 따라서 판매 증가와 함께 제품 구성의 변화, 
신규 차종의 초기 비용이 수익성에 미치는 영향을 살펴야 한다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 12 만 원으로 커버리지 개시 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 12 만 원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. 당사 12 개월 선행 EPS 에 
최근 1 년 평균 P/E 6.5 배를 적용했다. 연간 감익 부담은 남아 있지만, 지역별 수요에 
대응할 제품과 생산기반은 확대돼 있다. 낮아진 밸류에이션 부담을 감안하면, 이미 갖
춘 라인업의 판매 성과와 총주주환원율 35% 이상의 정책에 주목해야 한다는 판단이
다.

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기, 물량 감소에도 확인된 이익 방어력 
현대차의 3Q26 매출액은 45조 1,100억원(YoY -3.4%), 영업이익은 2조 6,770억원
(YoY +5.5%), OPM 5.9%(YoY +0.5%p)를 기록했다. 국내 파업 및 영업일수 감소 영
향으로 글로벌 도매 판매량은 92.1만대(YoY -11.3%)까지 감소했으나, HEV 중심의 
고부가 차종 Mix 개선, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비(YoY -1.2조원) 감소 효과
로 수익성은 오히려 개선됐다. 특히 HEV 판매 비중은 18.2%(YoY +2.7%p)까지 상
승했다. 반면 EV 판매는 전년 대비 감소세가 이어지며 지역별 수요 둔화와 경쟁 심
화 영향이 지속됐다. 
연간 눈높이는 낮아졌지만, 믹스 개선은 지속 
현대차는 글로벌 수요 둔화와 생산 차질을 반영해 2026년 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 
기존 415.8만대에서 400만대로, 영업이익률 가이던스를 기존 6.3~7.3%에서 
5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정했다. 다만 북미를 중심으로 HEV 판매가 견조하게 유지되고 
있으며, 하반기 주요 신차 투입과 전동화 라인업 확대를 통해 믹스 개선 기조는 이
어질 전망이다. 중장기적으로는 로보택시 공급 확대와 Atlas 상용화 등 Physical AI 
사업도 계획대로 진행 중으로, 단기 판매 성장 둔화에도 자동차 본업의 수익성 방어
와 신사업 모멘텀은 유효한 것으로 판단한다. 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 460,000원 하향 
글로벌 완성차 업체들의 수익성 부담은 확대되고 있다. 3분기 자동차 부문 영업이익
률은 Mercedes-Benz 2.5%, Stellantis 0.7%, Volvo -5.6%, VW -7.8%를 기록했다. 
유럽 내 BEV 판매 확대에도 중국 전기차 업체들의 공격적인 해외 진출과 가격 경
쟁, 중국 시장 부진이 동시에 나타나면서 전통 OEM의 수익성 압박이 심화되고 있
다. 이러한 환경에서 현대차는 HEV 중심의 유연한 파워트레인 전략과 북미 중심의 
Mix 개선을 통해 5% 후반의 수익성을 유지하고 있다는 점이 상대적으로 부각된다.

Document 260: AI制药终于开始省时间了

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI制药终于开始省时间了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

早期药物发现周期从3⾄4年缩短⾄2.5年以内，临床研究报告的90%由AI起草，审阅时间减
少25%。这是⼤型药企⾸次以量化数据证明，AI不再只是实验室⾥的概念，⽽是开始真实
地压缩研发时间与成本。摩根⼤通认为，在⼯具平⺠化的背景下，拥有数据资产与实验能
⼒的企业才具备可持续的竞争壁垒。
摘要：
据追⻛交易台，摩根⼤通在最新发布的研报中指出，诺华在11⽉19⽇的投资者⽇上⾸次系统披
露了AI在药物研发中的实际成效：早期药物发现周期从3⾄4年缩短⾄2.5年以内，临床研究报告
的90%由AI起草，审阅时间减少25%。这是⼤型药企⾸次以量化数据证明，AI不再只是实验室
⾥的概念，⽽是开始真实地压缩研发时间与成本。摩根⼤通认为，这⼀信号对AI制药（AIDD）
投资主题构成建设性⽀撑。
诺华管理层同时给出了审慎的⼀⾯：许多AI设计的分⼦仍在基础细胞测试中失败，AI尚未被证明
能提升⼈体临床试验的成功率。11⽉23⽇，诺华CEO Vas Narasimhan在接受CNBC采访时表
⽰，AI有望在未来5⾄10年内将药物发现与开发周期从12年压缩⾄8年，并将⼈体试验成功率从

8%提升⾄12%，但他明确反对“⼗年治愈癌症”的乐观论调，认为复杂的⼈体⽣物学决定了这⼀
⽬标过于激进。
摩根⼤通的解读是，AIDD的实际收益可能落在科技界的乐观预期与制药界的谨慎判断之间。研
报强调，算⼒与开源⼤模型正在让AI制药⼯具趋于同质化，真正的护城河是私有数据——诺华
依托其Data42平台积累了超过150PB、历时⼗年构建的实验室数据。这⼀判断对中国AIDD标的
构成正⾯信号，摩根⼤通更⻘睐将AI⼯具与⼤规模湿实验室数据相结合的本⼟ AIDD公司与CXO
企业。
研发前端：发现周期压缩近四成
诺华披露的最直观数据来⾃药物发现环节。
管理层称，早期药物发现时间已从3⾄4年降⾄2.5年以内，压缩幅度接近四成。这⼀环节历来是
制药⾏业最耗时、最依赖试错的部分，周期缩短意味着候选分⼦能够更早进⼊后续开发流程。
摩根⼤通在研报中写道，诺华管理层分享了明确迹象，显⽰AI已经在药物发现、临床试验和⼯
⼚运营三个环节削减成本、节省时间。该⾏认为，AI正从早期的测试案例转向真实的研发应
⽤，为效率提升和管线加速创造了空间。

不过研报也如实记录了管理层的保留意⻅：许多由AI设计的分⼦依然⽆法通过基础细胞测试。这
意味着前端时间的节省尚未转化为分⼦质量的提升，AI在发现环节的作⽤⽬前更多体现为筛选效
率，⽽⾮成功率。
临床与⽣产：报告起草与⼊组预测的量化改善
在临床试验环节，诺华给出的数据同样具体。AI⽬前承担了90%的临床研究报告起草⼯作，并将
报告审阅时间缩短25%；试验⼊组预测的准确率提升了50%。对于动辄涉及数千名患者、跨越
多个国家的⼤型临床试验⽽⾔，⼊组进度预测的准确性直接关系到试验周期与预算控制。
⽣产端的案例来⾃核药运输。在Pluvicto与Lutathera的配送中，AI⽀持实现了接近100%的准时
交付。核药具有半衰期短、物流窗⼝窄的特性，准时率的⼩幅提升对商业化供应意义重⼤。管
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

理层还提到，AI帮助削减了外部咨询费⽤，并降低了⼯⼚浪费。
这些环节的共同特征是流程标准化程度⾼、数据可得性强，因⽽成为AI最早落地的场景。摩根⼤
通认为，这类“早期胜利”表明AI正在获得真实的应⽤牵引⼒，但其价值集中在运营效率，⽽⾮科
学突破。
CEO的平衡判断：12年变8年，成功率8%变12%
诺华CEO Vas Narasimhan在11⽉23⽇的CNBC采访中给出了更⻓期的时间表。他表⽰，AI有望
在未来5⾄10年内将药物发现与开发的整体周期从12年缩短⾄8年，并将⼈体临床试验的成功率
从8%提升⾄12%。Narasimhan同时担任Anthropic董事会成员。
但他与部分科技界领袖的判断存在明显分歧。Narasimhan认为，受制于复杂的⼈体⽣物学，“⼗
年内治愈癌症”的⽬标过于乐观。他提出，AIDD的真实收益可能位于科技界的期望与制药界的谨
慎之间。
这⼀表态为市场提供了⼀个校准坐标：AI对研发效率的改善已有数据⽀撑，但对疾病治愈率的跃
升仍需时间验证。对投资者⽽⾔，前者对应可⻅的成本节约与管线提速，后者则属于尚⽆法定

价的期权。
数据才是护城河，中国标的受益
摩根⼤通研报的核⼼判断是，诺华投资者⽇与此前的罗⽒制药⽇⼀样，指向对AIDD的建设性展
望，并更⻘睐拥有真实数据与湿实验室规模的企业。
管理层提到，算⼒与开源⼤语⾔模型可能正在使AIDD⼯具趋于均等化，真正的优势来⾃内部数
据。诺华依托Data42平台，积累了超过150PB、历时⼗年构建的私有实验室数据。摩根⼤通认
为，这对中国AIDD股票构成正⾯信号，该⾏看好将AI⼯具与⼤型实验室数据集相结合的本⼟
AIDD公司与CXO企业。

### Page 3

摩根⼤通对AIDD的配置思路趋于精选：在⼯具平⺠化的背景下，拥有数据资产与实验能⼒的企
业才具备可持续的竞争壁垒。

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

► 今年以來適逢美伊衝突難題遲遲未解，高油
價讓聯準會重啟升息且堆高升息預期，面對
部分聯準會官員保持觀望立場，利率期貨顯
示聯準會將升息一碼且時點可能在12月，未
來1年則存在3碼潛在升息空間，考量地緣衝
突短期 內持 續， 若 通膨難 以改善 至政 策目
標，預期聯準會2027年將維持緊縮貨幣政策
的鷹派立場來對抗通膨，然美國2年期公債利
率已多反映未來升息預期，後續仍視美國核
心物價發展，引導短天期公債利率走向。
► 儘管全球利率大幅上升，然對於整體金融環
境影響甚微，反映美股和信用市場低波動狀
態，體現實質政策利率低於緊縮利率區間與
彭博金融情勢指數變化不大，當前美國貨幣
政策與市場流動性仍偏寬鬆，倘若地緣擾動
持續造成通膨居高難下，恐進一步推升聯準

會升息預期，疊加政府財政風險與貨幣政策
透明度降，加劇長天期公債利率上行風險，
長天期債以交易為主，不建議長期持有。

► 特斯拉3Q26財報好壞參半 — 毛利率與交付
量超預 期， 營收 大 致符合 ，營業 利潤 遜預
期，最大意外是自由現金流達15億美元，高
於共識負43億，資本開支指引下調成憂慮。
► 早前公司展示下代Roadster能力，本質上是
光環車，定價高受眾少，2028年才有望投
產，盈利貢獻有限。Model 3/Y成為大眾商
品，競爭激烈，利潤率壓縮。正如傳統品牌
以賽道或參賽展示技術，「飛天」旨在展示
獨有跨界整合能力，維持品牌形象溢價。
► 至於和SpaceX的潛在合併，核心在於貫通多
頂 業 務 ， 提 高 資 本 運 用 效 率 ， 共同分擔
Terafab的開支等，現時兩家合計市值約為
3.5兆美元。這項巨型潛在交易，除了要跨過
極高表決門檻外，SpaceX具極敏感的美國國
防及NASA合作背景，而特斯拉有超過20%
業務倚重上海超級工廠，合併存在嚴重地緣
衝突。預期馬斯克將在川普離任前，加速執
行Tesla與SpaceX 合併。

► 今年9月適逢地緣擾動持續推升高油價、聯準
會重啟 升息 、財政部與企 業發債 浪潮 等影
響，形塑債市今年以來單月最差表現。檢視
2021年以來美國投資級債前十大單月最差總
報酬，2022年聯準會3月啟動升息年度就佔
一半以上，顯示利率風險仍為近5年來債市最
大風險，然檢視單月最差報酬表現未來期間
總報酬，除2022年1月和3月聯準會重啟升息
前夕與當月表現明顯較弱，多數於未來1年後
多呈現正報酬，反映隨利率走升放大孳息效
益，同時挹注債券長期持有之報酬空間。
► 今年來債券市場利率波動明顯推升信用債利
差，其 中僅 以低 信 評債利 差升幅 較顯 ，此
外，9月以來利率連動度高及AI相關產業債種
也成為利差擴大重災區，凸顯債市投資人減
輕市場不確定性的投資傾向，考量信用債市
歷經利率上行利空洗禮，信用利差同步擴大

創造收益空間，可把握債信體質仍佳企業債
鎖利良機，並透過時間複利力量強化收益。
利率波動升，僅低信評利差擴大程度相對明顯9月為美國投資級債今年最差單月報酬
用時間換空間，單月最差表現形塑未來報酬機會

### Page 8

► 受聯準會重啟升息影響，過去一年美國公債
殖利率曲線持續陡峭化，其中利率最大升幅
落在短天期的2至3年期間，美國核心通膨與
就業仍具韌性，聯準會將持續保持緊縮貨幣
政策步調來應對高通膨，考量美國高物價與
高利率環境短期難消，宜透過縮短債券配置
天期並進行動態調整，為因應後續利率不確
定性環境的良策。
► 為因應利率不確定性，投資人可採取債券梯
的投資配置，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，債券梯投資重點主要在
於，根據殖利率曲線型態及利率動態建立不
同期間債券梯，假設市場利率走升且殖利率
曲線正斜率，建立短天期債券梯除了降低利
率風險，有 短期 閒 置資金 及孳息 需求 投資
人，亦可取得穩定孳息收益。若後續投資人
有短期資金需求，可以視情況將短期債券到
期後資金回收，降低只投資單一債券須持至
到期或直接變現的流動性風險。

高利率波動持續，用策略迎戰市場擾動
資料來源：Bloomberg，凱基證券整理

債券梯
經過第2年第一檔債券到期，視資金需求及市況與利率動
態決定後續配置

短天期債券梯：假設買入2檔各3、4、5及7年到期債券，
聚焦短期穩定收益
► 債券梯是一種分散投資方式，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，可兼具：
1) 分散利率風險：將資金分別買進不同到期年期的債券，以分
散利率風險。
2) 分散信用風險：分散投資不同發行機構債券，不會重壓單一
債券並可分散信用違約風險。
3) 降低流動性風險：若投資人有短期資金需求，可以視情況將
短期債券到期後資金回收，降低只投資單一債券須持至到期
或直接變現之流動性風險。

### Page 9

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長，市場靜待財報結果與展望，和對AI資本支出回收影響。
考量地緣衝突、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉，短期金融市場紛擾不止，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變，逢股市拉回時可分批布局，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間，殖利率偏高環境使中短天期債券提供
吸引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後
利差具吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。

 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高，使金價短期承壓，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。

### Page 18

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

債、非投等債及新興債全數上漲。黃金則是
受惠避險資金低接買盤而從低點大幅回升。
► 歐元表現相對具有支撐，歐元區10月消費者
物價指數年增率攀升至4.0%，為三年來最
高，主要受能源成本所推動，市場預期歐洲
央行或將於12月再度升息支撐歐元，日圓方
面，日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，
未如市場預期偏鷹，令日圓持續弱勢盤整。

熱門議題
► 黃金價格歷經第二季修整，近日呈現大幅反
彈走勢，主要係因美國期中選舉後，市場憂
心AI產業監管將趨嚴，加上晶片與科技股遭
遇大幅修正，促使部分資金轉往黃金等防禦
型資產尋求避險。
► 根據世界黃金協會最新報告，第3季全球央行
淨買進321公噸黃金，較第二季明顯回升，年
增率達42%，創下自2024年第四季以來的單
季新高，前3季累計買進金額更是高達675公
噸，顯示黃金儲備已經列入全球央行必需資
產配置而且比重維持穩定增長態勢。
► 長期以來黃金具有抗通膨特性，觀察黃金與
聯邦基金利率走勢，從2020年之後，即便全
球歷經2次升息循環，黃金價格仍呈現穩健上
揚走勢，反應其抗通膨保值特性。此外，美
國財政赤字壓力難消，恐令美元長期購買力
被稀釋， 加 速 全 球 央 行 去 美 元 化 ， 有 鑑 於
此，建議黃金納入資產配置。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，凱基證券整理；註：右上產業回報相關性為各產業過去3年之每週報酬相關性
► 醫療類股於美國第三季財報展現強勁的獲利
韌性，實際獲利優於預期程度為主要產業中
表現最佳，背後主要受惠大型製藥企業(如：
禮來、再生元製藥、吉利德科學)在減重藥、
癌症治療及創新藥領域之需求持續增長，帶
動整體產業獲利表現優於市場預期。
► 期中選舉後醫療政策輪廓逐漸明朗。民主黨
主張支持擴大醫療保險覆蓋、延續平價醫療
法案補貼以及緩和醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)
削減，有助提升醫療服務使用率；雖藥價談
判政策恐帶來部分定價壓力，但整體政策主
張仍對創新藥、生技研發及醫療保險產業具
潛在政策支持效果。
► 綜上，在AI投資回報率面臨市場檢驗之際，
市場資金持續尋求防禦性配置。醫療保健類
股與資訊科技類股相關性偏低，有助分散投
資組合對單一AI題材依賴，加上第三季獲利
動能轉強、創新藥需求穩健及產業能見度提
升，後續有望持續受惠資金輪動效應。

財報與政策題材雙利多，醫療類股成市場新寵兒

政策議題 民主黨傾向 共和黨傾向 兩黨共識對製藥產業潛在影響
降低藥價 依《通膨削減法案》框架，擴大醫療 補助計劃(Medicaid)藥價談判適用範圍 傾向透過最惠國定價機制來降低藥 價 兩黨均主張降低藥價 製藥企業面臨定價壓力
美國本土製造 透過研發補助、稅收優惠及產業政策 鼓勵藥廠回流美國生產 透過關稅、供應鏈重組及企業承諾 推動製藥產能回流 兩黨均支持醫藥供應鏈自主化 有利本土藥廠及原料藥供應鏈投資

大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)覆蓋範圍，
並緩和該補助計劃的削減
傾向由州政府及市場機制主導醫療
體系，限制聯邦政府支出
醫療的可負擔性為兩黨政策共同焦點，有助
增加就醫人口及處方藥使用量，支撐製藥、

投資焦點必需消費
► 短期而言，檢視各類股在最近股災表現，美
國成長股、資訊科技、與通訊服務等高波動
度類股，表現明顯落後大盤，其中，資訊科
技與通訊服務波動度最大、跌幅最重，主要
係因美股科技巨頭財報不如預期、負自由現
金流量，疊加期中選舉後民主黨恐緊縮AI基
礎建設進程及資本支出見頂等不確定因素，
令市場 擔憂 導致 資 金流出 ；並轉 進核 心消
費、健康護理、防禦股、公用事業等防禦性
產業，報酬相對較穩健，受市場資金青睞。
► 長期而言，從2008迄今，核心消費類股長期
表現穩健上漲。與主要指數相比，面對近月
AI科技股修正，同期間費半大跌18%、那斯
達克跌幅6%、標普跌幅1%，而核心消費類
股大漲13%，反應其低波動、抗震韌性。
► 整體而言，從2015年至今，歷經2次通膨與
升息循環，核心消費受影響性低，有鑑於資
產配置角度，建議短期跟隨資金流向配置核
心消費股，提高投組抗震韌性。
必需消費長期表現穩健、抗通膨、低波動避險資金流入，必需消費類股單月表現最佳

配置必需消費類股，提高投組抗震韌性

股市
 隨著美國期中選舉結束，美伊衝突可望降溫，市場關注焦點將回歸基本面，惟AI資料中心建設進程
或將受到州政府緊縮政策而造成評價面壓力增添股價波動。短期可布局防禦股、分散配置於不同國
家與產業，增強抗震力、降低市場波動影響，長期而言，AI從全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高
的品質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨勢不變。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高的品
質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨
勢不變，非AI部分可關注必需消費產業、
醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 隨資金湧入，公債殖利率回落，惟根據最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升
息空間，立場偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走升
時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引

力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 聯準會12月份會議升息態度偏鷹，美元高檔整理。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理，日圓弱勢盤整
商品
 美國期中選舉結束，預期中東地緣衝突降溫，油價從近期高點回落，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法
